Copa del Rey 2003/04

Played by 81 teams:
 - all 20 clubs of First Division. 
 - 21 (out of 22) clubs of Second Division. Málaga B couldn't inscribe.
 - 28 (out of 80) clubs of Second Division B.
     - 3 (out of 3), relegated from Second A the previous season.
     - 19 which were in Second B the previous season: 
            22 teams qualified (but 3 had been promoted)as top 5 of each group and 
            the next clubs (no "reserve teams") of any group with more points.
            (Reserve teams excluded: Real Madrid B, Athletic Bilbao B, Alavés B, Barcelona B and Valencia B)
     - 6 which were champions of their group of Third Div. AND had been promoted
 - 12 (out of 342, in 17 groups) of Third Division:
     - 11 champions of Third Division which didn't reach promotion (one of them, Baskonia, substitued by Zalla)
     - R Oviedo, who has been relegated from Second to Second B and had an additional administrative relegation.
